RAJYA SABHA SECRETARIAT 

New Delhi, the 4th August 1972 

Nti RSpUil) /12-Pet sonnel . — The Chairman, Rajya 
Sabha, has been pleaded to confirm Sh-ri p. K. Das, perma- 
nent Section Officer and officiating Under Secretary in the 
Rajya Sabha Secretariat, in the post of Under Secretary in 
that Secretariat, with effect from the 15th May, 1972, 

No, RS /3 ( ill) /UPersonncl.— The Chairman, Rajya Sabha 
has been pleased to confirm Shri P, Venkatachalam, perma- 
nent Assistant Private Secretary and officiating Private Secre- 
tary to Chairman, in the post of Private Secretary to Chair- 
man, with effect from the 17th April, 1972. 

No. RS/2( vii) /12-Personnel .- — Shri Dewan Chand, perma- 
nent Assistant Editor, who was appointed to officiate as 
Editor of Debates (Hindi) vice Shri O. C. Pandey, Editor 
of Debates (Hindi) granted leave, vide this Secretariat noti- 
fication of even number dated the 12th June, 1972, has, with 
effect from 21-7-72, been temporarily allowed to officiate as 
Editor of Debates (English) vice Shri V. S. Nanda, Editor 
of Debates (English), on leave. 

No. RS/2(vii) /12-Personnel.— Shri Dewan Chand who 
was allowed to officiate as Editor of Debates (English), vide 
notification of even number dated the 31st July, 1972, revert- 
ed to his substantive post of Assistant Editor with effect 
from the afternoon of the 29th July, 1972. 

M, KAMESWARA RAO, Under Secy.
